## Latest handoff - ScenePlaybackPlan JSON payload bridge

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim v2 browser QA payload bridge for Manim `Scene.play/wait` lifecycle planning: `pre_play -> begin_animations -> progress_through_animations -> finish_animations -> post_play`, plus wait-frame updater behavior.
- Result:
  - `mathScenePlayback.ts` now exports `serializeScenePlaybackPlan()` for deterministic, script-safe JSON embedding of playback plan steps, lifecycle phases, time progression, play compilation, begin/progress/finish evidence, wait control, skipping window, pre-play control, and post-play preview.
  - `mathScenePlayback.test.ts` now verifies playback-plan JSON parseability / `<` escaping in addition to lifecycle plan construction, time progression, wait/presenter behavior, skipping, pre/post play, begin/progress/finish metadata, sampled frames, run-from-beat times, event streams, summaries, and renderer-independence behavior.
  - `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx` now serializes `manimPlaybackPlan` and exposes:
    - `data-viz-manim-playback-plan`
    - `data-viz-manim-playback-json`
  - `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` includes both playback payload selectors immediately after playback control.
  - `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts` guards the playback source contract, serializer, Canvas import/call, memo, JSON script, and required selector coverage.
  - `mathSceneSmokeHook.test.ts` includes `[data-viz-manim-playback-json]` in the ordered JSON payload selector list.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts` smoke-hook expectations now reflect 243 selectors and 107 JSON payloads while keeping 1574 root data attributes.

## Manim render-quality contract slice

- Scope: A06/S06 pure MAIS Manim framework layer under `components/visualizations/three/manim/`.
- Motivation: source-contract scan showed `save_state`, updater execution, frame stepping, playback, capture, and file-writer semantics already existed; the next useful gap was a dedicated Manim-style output quality contract for 3Blue1Brown-level visual polish.
- Changes:
  - Added `mathSceneRenderQuality.ts` and `mathSceneRenderQuality.test.ts`.
  - New pure contract normalizes Manim-style `pixel_width`, `pixel_height`, `frame_rate`, transparent background, DPR, antialiasing, and sampling into a deterministic `MathSceneRenderQualityPlan`.
  - Presets cover `preview`, `interactive`, `hd`, `production`, and `fourk`.
  - Interactive mode uses viewport dimensions while preserving capture/export defaults; capture mode locks to preset output dimensions.
  - Added stable browser QA attribute mapping via `renderQualityDataAttributes`.
  - Updated `mathSceneCapture.ts` so capture planning can consume a render-quality plan as default width/height/fps for screenshot/video export.

## Latest handoff - Mobject.next_to layout evidence bridge

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim v2 Mobject layout source-distillation slice.
- Result:
  - `mathMobjectLayout.ts` now supports Manim-style `Mobject.next_to` alongside existing `Mobject.arrange` and `Mobject.align_to`.
  - Added `buildMobjectNextToLayoutPlan()` with deterministic critical-point placement: source opposite critical point -> target selected critical point + normalized direction gap.
  - The pure layout plan records `layoutKind: "nextTo"`, `nextTargetId`, `gap`, normalized direction, finite deltas, source/target bounding boxes, missing source/target diagnostics, stable signature, and script-safe JSON.

  - `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx` and `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` now expose/require the new `next_to` layout QA attributes while keeping existing arrange/align_to evidence stable.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.ts` widened `mobjectLayoutKind` to `"arrange" | "alignTo" | "nextTo"` so future evidence snapshots can carry all three Manim layout primitives.

## Formula SVG morph runtime overlay bridge

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim v2 FormulaLayer and scene-spec runtime bridge.
- Motivation: Manim source distillation treats `Tex`/`SVGMobject` as vector path objects that can morph like other VMobjects. MAIS already had a pure SVG path morph subsystem and browser evidence, but authored morph specs were not part of `MathSceneSpec`, FormulaLayer only consumed morphs through explicit options, and `MathFormulaOverlay` only emitted hidden morph markers rather than a visible runtime path frame.
- Changes:
  - `mathSceneTypes.ts`: added `FormulaSvgPathMorphSceneSpec` and optional `formulaSvgMorphs` on `MathSceneSpec`.
  - `mathFormulaLayer.ts`: now consumes `scene.formulaSvgMorphs` by default while preserving explicit `svgPathMorphs` override support.
  - `mathTexCacheManifest.ts` and `mathFormulaSvgMorphEvidence.ts`: now use scene-authored morph specs when options are not provided, keeping cache/evidence/runtime on one authored source.
  - Added `mathFormulaSvgMorphRuntime.ts` and `mathFormulaSvgMorphRuntime.test.ts`: builds deterministic runtime frames from scene-authored SVG morph specs, interpolates paths by timeline progress, reports compatible frame counts, issue counts, path preview, and `FORMULA_SVG_MORPH_RUNTIME_SOURCE_CONTRACT`.
  - `MathFormulaOverlay.tsx`: builds SVG morph runtime state using `runtimeState?.timeline.easedLocalProgress`, exposes `data-viz-manim-svg-morph-runtime-*` QA attrs, and renders compatible morph frames as fixed-in-frame `<svg><path d={frame.path} /></svg>` inside the formula layer.
  - `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` and `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts`: added smoke-checkable overlay selectors `data-viz-manim-svg-morph-runtime-frame` and `data-viz-manim-svg-morph-runtime-source-contract`.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts`: smoke-hook selector count updated from `82` to `84`; attribute count remains `1516` and JSON payload count remains `27`.

## TeX color-map runtime FormulaLayer bridge

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim v2 formula layer under `components/visualizations/three/manim/`.
- Motivation: Manim source distillation says `Tex`/`StringMobject` supports `tex_to_color_map`, `t2c`, and isolation selectors. MAIS already exposed TeX color-map and isolation evidence, but the fixed formula overlay still rendered the original `formula.latex`; semantic token colors were visible only through token chips/data attrs, not the main KaTeX formula.
- Changes:
  - Added `mathTexColorizedFormula.ts` and `mathTexColorizedFormula.test.ts`.
  - New pure adapter builds a deterministic colorized formula string by injecting KaTeX-compatible `\color{#hex}{...}` commands for formula tokens whose text or plain Greek alias can be matched in the LaTeX body.
  - Adapter reports colored/uncolored token ids, role summary, interval summary, and `TEX_COLORIZED_FORMULA_SOURCE_CONTRACT` so QA can distinguish tokens truly rendered in the main formula from semantic-only bindings.
  - `mathTexColorMap.ts`: exported `texColorHexForRole` so render-time color injection and evidence use the same role-to-hex mapping.
  - `MathFormulaOverlay.tsx`: feeds `colorizedFormula.latex` into `MathText` instead of raw `formula.latex` and exposes `data-viz-manim-tex-colorized-*` QA attrs.
  - `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts`: added `data-viz-manim-tex-colorized-source-contract` as a smoke-checkable overlay selector.
  - `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts` and `mathFormulaLayer.test.ts`: guard overlay consumption of `buildTexColorizedFormula`, the KaTeX input bridge, data attrs, source contract, and renderer-independent helper purity.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts`: smoke-hook selector count updated from `81` to `82`; attribute count remains `1516`.

## ShowCreation partial-stroke evidence

- Scope: A06/S06 Manim `ShowCreation` / `VMobject.pointwise_become_partial` source-contract evidence.
- Motivation: the creation primitive plan already counted `showCreation`, but browser QA did not yet expose the Manim source-level semantics that `ShowCreation` reveals a VMobject stroke by displaying the partial path `[0, alpha]`.
- Changes:
  - Added `mathShowCreationEvidence.ts` and `mathShowCreationEvidence.test.ts`.
  - New pure evidence builder filters `showCreation` primitives, samples deterministic progress frames, records draw ranges `0.000..alpha`, opacity, object ids, phase sequence, source contract, and partial-stroke policy.
  - `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx`: builds ShowCreation evidence from the existing creation primitive plan and exposes 10 `data-viz-manim-show-creation-*` root QA attributes.
  - `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` and `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts`: require the 10 public ShowCreation attrs and guard Canvas wiring plus source-contract constants.

## Capture/file-writer render-quality metadata propagation

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim export evidence, capture planning, SceneFileWriter-style manifests, Canvas QA attributes, and evidence harness.
- Motivation: render-quality settings were visible on Canvas and influenced R3F DPR/background/capture defaults, but the downstream `MathSceneCapturePlan` and `MathSceneFileWriterPlan` did not persist the selected fps, preset, renderer mode, DPR, samples, background color, alpha, or transparency settings. That left screenshot/video export evidence weaker than Manim's source-level `SceneFileWriter`/camera-config audit trail.
- Changes:
  - `mathSceneCapture.ts`: added deterministic capture metadata fields for `fps`, render-quality preset, renderer mode, DPR, samples, transparent background, background color, and alpha; `capturePlanDataAttributes()` now exposes the same values through `data-viz-manim-capture-*` attributes.
  - `mathSceneFileWriterPlan.ts`: propagates capture dimensions and render-quality metadata into the SceneFileWriter-style plan and `data-viz-manim-file-writer-capture-*` attributes.
  - `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx`: root runtime attributes now expose capture quality metadata and file-writer capture quality metadata for browser QA.
  - `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts` and `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts`: shared smoke contract now requires the new capture/file-writer quality attributes.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.ts`: evidence snapshots now store capture quality metadata and file-writer capture quality metadata; `evidenceDataAttributes()` reconstructs the expanded attribute set.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts`: verifies quality metadata through snapshot fields and attributes; smoke-hook attribute count updated from 1428 to 1446 after adding 18 QA attributes.

## Render-quality-aware frame audit

## Fade/Grow runtime opacity and scale bridge

- Scope: A06/S06 actual runtime semantics for Manim creation/fading/growing primitives.
- Motivation: `mathFadeGrowEvidence.ts` already exposed Manim source evidence for `FadeIn`, `FadeOut`, and `GrowFromCenter`, but runtime objects still mainly relied on membership to appear/disappear. The source-level behavior should be visible in the actual MathObject graph: FadeIn/FadeOut interpolate opacity, and GrowFromCenter scales geometry from its center while fading in.
- Changes:
  - `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`: added `applyActiveFadeGrowFrame`, using `buildCreationPrimitiveFrame` so runtime opacity/scale follows the same pure primitive semantics as the evidence layer.
  - `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`: added center-scale helpers based on `mapRuntimeRenderState`; the scaled render state recomputes bounding boxes from current runtime points.
  - `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`: applies active fade/grow frames even when ordinary family updaters are suspended by Manim-style animation ownership.
  - `mathSceneRuntimeState.test.ts`: added a deterministic vector scene proving `FadeIn` opacity `0.25`, `GrowFromCenter` opacity/center-scale `0.25`, `FadeOut` opacity `0.75`, and final fade-out opacity `0`.

## DrawBorderThenFill runtime and filled-surface render evidence

- Scope: A06/S06 MAIS Manim v2 Manim-source runtime bridge for filled sampled surfaces.
- Motivation: `DrawBorderThenFill` evidence already described Manim's source behavior, but `revealSurface` runtime still only clipped the surface wireframe by progress. Filled surfaces therefore did not express the Manim lifecycle where the outline is drawn first with fill opacity forced to zero, then the fill/style interpolates in.
- Changes:
  - `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`: added an authored-style `DrawBorderThenFill` frame path for active `revealSurface` steps when a surface has positive authored `fillOpacity`.
  - `mathUpdaterRegistry.ts`: surface reveal now uses the creation frame `drawRange[1]` for partial wireframe progress and the creation frame style for `fillOpacity`, preserving ordinary ShowCreation-like wireframe behavior for surfaces without fill.
  - `mathSceneRuntimeState.test.ts`: added deterministic runtime coverage proving border phase `fillOpacity=0`, fill phase restores/interpolates fill opacity, and surface wireframe rows follow the Manim frame draw range.
  - `mathVMobjectRenderStyle.ts`: added pure filled-surface mesh render helpers that triangulate surface render state into deterministic positions and combine VMobject fill opacity with Mobject material uniforms.
  - `MathSceneRuntime.tsx`: renders a filled surface mesh before the wireframe when fill mesh triangles are present, using `THREE.DoubleSide`, semantic fill color roles, material opacity, transparency, clipping planes, and depth-write policy.
  - `mathVMobjectRenderStyle.ts` and `mathEvidenceHarness.ts`: added `VMOBJECT_SURFACE_FILL_RENDER_SOURCE_CONTRACT` plus `data-viz-vmobject-render-fill-*` evidence for mesh object count, ids, triangle/vertex count, opacity range, roles, transparency, and summary.
  - `ThreeDLabCanvas.tsx`, `threeDCanvasSurfaceContract.ts`, and `threeDCanvasContract.test.ts`: exposed the 11 new fill-render QA attributes on the canvas surface.
  - `mathEvidenceHarness.test.ts`: smoke-hook attribute count updated from `1505` to `1516`.
